}

### 发言人改名（全局档案映射/局部句段改写，幂等）

- **接口ID**: 51783
- **分类**: AI速记
- **请求方式**: `POST`
- **路径**: `/gateway/livestream/api/aisteno/speaker/rename`
- **状态**: undone
- **维护人**: chao_sw_wu
- **更新时间**: 2026-08-19 20:20:01
- **标签**: AI速记

**Headers**

| name | value | required(必填) | desc |
|---|---|---|---|
| Content-Type | application/json |  |  |

**请求 Body (json)**

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "scope",
    "stenoId"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "scope": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "操作类型：GLOBAL-全局改名 / LOCAL-局部修正 / MERGE-合并发言人"
    },
    "seq": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int32",
      "description": "目标句段顺序号（LOCAL 必填）"
    },
    "speakerId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "段表 speakerId（GLOBAL 必填；MERGE 必填，表示句段被划走的来源发言人）"
    },
    "stenoId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "速记ID"
    },
    "toName": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "新显示名（GLOBAL 必填）"
    },
    "toSpeakerId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "句段划入的目标 speakerId（LOCAL/MERGE 必填，可为现有或手动新建的发言人）"
    },
    "userId": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "绑定的企业用户ID（GLOBAL 可选，通讯录选人时传入）"
    }
  },
  "$$ref": "#/definitions/发言人改名请求"
}
```

**响应** (json)

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "data": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "affectedSegments": {
          "type": "integer",
          "format": "int64",
          "description": "受影响段数（GLOBAL=该发言人全部段数；LOCAL=1；幂等跳过=0）"
        }
      },
      "$$ref": "#/definitions/发言人改名响应"
    },
    "error": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "errorCode": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int32"
    },
    "success": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "timestamp": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    }
  },
  "$$ref": "#/definitions/Result«发言人改名响应»"
}
```

{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "data": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "hasSegmentData": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "example": false,
          "description": "是否存在段表数据（false=老数据仅纯文本全文，speakers 为空，前端隐藏入口）"
        },
        "hasTimestamp": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "example": false,
          "description": "是否存在有效时间戳（false=段无起止时间，segments/ratio 不可用）"
        },
        "speakers": {
          "type": "array",
          "description": "发言人列表（按首次发言顺序，末尾为手动新建的 0 段发言人）；无段数据或无说话人标识时为空列表",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "chars": {
                "type": "integer",
                "format": "int64",
                "description": "总字数"
              },
              "name": {
                "type": "string",
                "description": "显示名：已改名取发言人档案，未改名默认\"发言人N\"（N 按首次出现序）"
              },
              "ratio": {
                "type": "number",
                "format": "double",
                "description": "时长占比（0-1，= 该人发言时长/会议总时长；无时间戳时为 0）"
              },
              "segments": {
                "type": "array",

                "items": {
                  "type": "object",

                    },

                      "type": "integer",

                    }
                  },

                }
              },

              "turns": {
                "type": "integer",
                "format": "int32",
                "description": "句段数"
              },
              "userId": {
                "type": "string",
                "description": "绑定的企业用户ID（通讯录选人后有值，前端据此渲染真实头像）"
              }
            },
            "$$ref": "#/definitions/发言人画像项"
          }
        }
      },

    },

    },
    "success": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    }
  },
  "$$ref": "#/definitions/Result«发言人画像响应»"
}
